# All federal official sources: DRAFT claims

**Status:** DRAFT — every claim needs a licensed reviewer's approval in the app before it goes live

**Captured:** 2026-10-25T15:57:22.416Z (2026-10-25 10:57 CDT). Each page record has its own `retrievedAt`.

**Captured by:** scripts/ops/capture-all-sources.mts, run from Francis's agent box (Node fetch, Chrome User-Agent, 2.5 s between requests); IRCC fee list and live Justice Laws dates read through WebFetch; law text from Justice Canada's official XML. Aligned to the #248 format by align-to-248.py.

**Excluded by design:** Processing times (staff enter them). Provincial pages. Claims already on main: the 55 canada.ca claims shipped in #248 (captured-canada-ca.json) and the 20 in captured-claims.json.

**Totals:** 818 claims from 134 pages and documents, plus the IRCC fee list read through WebFetch; 17 items not captured; 12 fetch errors (all are HTTP 404 form pages); 0 URL-check problems.

Unknown stays Unknown. No sample or placeholder values. Each claim has an exact `sourceUrl` with a section anchor or named section, plus a verbatim excerpt.

## Provenance notes

- **canada.ca pages, forms, guides and checklist PDFs:** fetched from the box. Each record stores the sha256 of the bytes, the HTTP status, the final URL, dateModified and the printed dates.
- **Fee list (ircc.canada.ca):** the box's TLS connection to it is reset, so it was read through the WebFetch provider. Every amount was then checked against canada.ca's `fees.json` (real bytes, hashed). Re-fetch it in CI to get a page hash.
- **Law:** laws-lois.justice.gc.ca is also reset from the box. The text comes from Justice Canada's official XML (`github.com/justicecanada/laws-lois-xml`, main branch). The hash is of the XML bytes, and `currentTo` and `lastAmended` are the XML's `lims` dates.
  - The live site dates were read through WebFetch and are stored in `liveSite`. For example, the live IRPR is current to 2026-09-30, while the XML current-date is 2026-07-21. Re-check against the live site before approval.
  - Not-in-force and related provisions are dropped, because only `<Body>` is parsed.
- **Checklists:** static XFA tables are read row by row and kept word for word. Hybrid PDFs are captured as verbatim blocks under their printed headings, not as numbered items.
- **Fee placeholders:** 11 page and guide sections show “Error loading fee” in the raw HTML, because the amounts are loaded by script. Each one carries a reviewer note to take the amount from the `CA.FEES.*` claims.
- **Overlaps:** the 55 claims from #242 and Claude's 20 in `captured-claims.json` were skipped, so this file contains no duplicates of them.

## Aligned to #248

- captured-canada-ca.json format on main 201a141 (#248): CA.PROGRAM.* keys, category form|checklist|program (plus guide, fee, law), formNumber, sha256Of, modifiedOn from <gcds-date-modified>.
- canada.ca HTML hashes are point-in-time hashes over the raw bytes, as #248 documents for its own pages. The server's Add claim fetch re-hashes with hashableBytes.
- Dropped while aligning: CA.PAGE.MEDICAL.TEMPORARY.INTRO (intro is only the page's sub-navigation); CA.PAGE.MEDICAL.PERMANENT.INTRO (intro is only the page's sub-navigation); CA.PAGE.REP.AUTHORIZED.INTRO (same text as CA.PROGRAM.REPRESENTATIVE.AUTHORIZED, shipped in #248).

# Gap run (appended)

**Captured:** 2026-10-25T17:54:14.023Z (2026-10-25 12:54 CDT). **Script:** scripts/ops/capture-gaps2.mts, then merge-gaps2.py. **How:** Francis's agent box, Node fetch, Chrome User-Agent, 2.5 s between requests. HTML hashed with hashableBytes (Akamai mPulse script removed) as on main; PDFs hashed as received. Dates from <gcds-date-modified> / readPageDates; anything not printed is Unknown.

**Excluded:** Processing times. Provincial pages. Express Entry round values (rounds pages captured only as dated page claims; per-round ministerial instructions skipped). Anything already in all-sources-claims.json, captured-canada-ca.json (#248), captured-claims.json or #242. “Error loading fee” sections and sections stating an IRCC processing time (see excludedSections).

**New claims:** 1279. **Total now:** 2097. The earlier interrupted gap run had appended nothing (the file still had 818 claims), so nothing was duplicated.
